## vcs_tag()

This command detects revision control commit information at build time
and places it in the specified output file. This file is guaranteed to
be up to date on every build. Keywords are similar to [[custom_target]].

Meson will read the contents of `input`, substitute the
`replace_string` with the detected revision number, and write the
result to `output`. This method returns a
[[@custom_tgt]] object that (as usual) should be
used to signal dependencies if other targets use the file outputted
by this.

For example, if you generate a header with this and want to use that
in a build target, you must add the return value to the sources of
that build target. Without that, Meson will not know the order in
which to build the targets.

If you desire more specific behavior than what this command provides,
you should use [[custom_target]].

<p style="padding: 5px; margin: 0px;"></p> <!-- A bit of space because we remove the top margin below -->
<div style="display: flex;">
  <h3 style="margin-top: 0px;">Signature</h3>
  <div style="flex-grow: 1;"></div>
  <div class="d-flex flex-column align-items-center gap-1">

</div>
</div>

<pre><code class="language-meson"># This command detects revision control commit information at build time
[[#@custom_tgt]] vcs_tag(
  <a href="#vcs_tag_command"><b>command</b></a>        : [[#@array]][[[#@exe]] | [[#@program]] | [[#@custom_tgt]] | [[#@file]] | [[#@str]]]                                                                                                                                                                                   # The command to execute, see [[custom_target]] for details
  <a href="#vcs_tag_fallback"><b>fallback</b></a>       : [[#@str]]                                                                                                                                                                                                                              # Version number to use when no revision control information is present,
  <a href="#vcs_tag_input"><b>input</b></a>          : [[#@str]]                                                                                                                                                                                                                  <i>[required]</i>  # File to modify (e
  <a href="#vcs_tag_install"><b>install</b></a>        : [[#@bool]]                                                                                                                                                                                                                             # When true, this generated file is installed during
  <a href="#vcs_tag_install_dir"><b>install_dir</b></a>    : [[#@str]]                                                                                                                                                                                                                              # The subdirectory to install the generated file to (e
  <a href="#vcs_tag_install_mode"><b>install_mode</b></a>   : [[#@array]][[[#@str]] | [[#@int]]]                                                                                                                                                                                                                 # Specify the file mode in symbolic format
  <a href="#vcs_tag_install_tag"><b>install_tag</b></a>    : [[#@str]]                                                                                                                                                                                                                              # A string used by the `meson install --tags` command
  <a href="#vcs_tag_output"><b>output</b></a>         : [[#@str]]                                                                                                                                                                                                                  <i>[required]</i>  # File to write the results to (e
  <a href="#vcs_tag_replace_string"><b>replace_string</b></a> : [[#@str]]                                                                                                                                                                                                                              # String in the input file to substitute with the commit information
)</code></pre>




  <p style="padding: 5px; margin: 0px;"></p> <!-- A bit more space -->

  ### Arguments




  The function `vcs_tag()`
  accepts the following keyword arguments:

  <table class="table table-bordered table-sm">
    <thead>
      <tr>
        <th class="text-center text-nowrap">Name</th>
        <th class="text-center text-nowrap">Type</th>
        <th>Description</th>
      </tr>
    </thead>
    <tbody>
      <tr id="vcs_tag_command">
        <td class="text-center text-nowrap">
  
  <code class="language-meson">command</code>
  </td>
        <td class="text-center">
  
  [[@array]][<span class="text-nowrap">[[@exe]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@program]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@custom_tgt]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@file]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span>]
  </td>
        <td>
  
  The command to execute, see [[custom_target]] for details
on how this command must be specified.

This parameter is optional. If it is absent, Meson will try
its best to find a suitable default command.

*(since 0.62.0)* [[@file]] is accepted.

*(since 0.63.0)* [[@custom_tgt]], [[@exe]], and [[@program]] are accepted.

  </td>
      </tr>
      <tr id="vcs_tag_fallback">
        <td class="text-center text-nowrap">
  
  <code class="language-meson">fallback</code>
  </td>
        <td class="text-center">
  
  <span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span>
  <br><code class="language-meson text-nowrap">default = [[meson.project_version]]</code>
  </td>
        <td>
  
  Version number to use when no revision control information is present,
such as when building from a release tarball.

  </td>
      </tr>
      <tr id="vcs_tag_input">
        <td class="text-center text-nowrap">
  
  <code class="language-meson">input</code>
  </td>
        <td class="text-center">
  
  <span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span>
  </td>
        <td>
  
  File to modify (e.g. `version.c.in`).
  </td>
      </tr>
      <tr id="vcs_tag_install">
        <td class="text-center text-nowrap">
  
  <code class="language-meson">install</code>
  <br>{meson:since}`1.7.0`
  </td>
        <td class="text-center">
  
  <span class="text-nowrap">[[@bool]]</span>
  <br><code class="language-meson text-nowrap">default = false</code>
  </td>
        <td>
  
  When true, this generated file is installed during
the install step, and `install_dir` must be set and not empty.

  </td>
      </tr>
      <tr id="vcs_tag_install_dir">
        <td class="text-center text-nowrap">
  
  <code class="language-meson">install_dir</code>
  <br>{meson:since}`1.7.0`
  </td>
        <td class="text-center">
  
  <span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span>
  </td>
        <td>
  
  The subdirectory to install the generated file to (e.g. `share/myproject`).

  </td>
      </tr>
      <tr id="vcs_tag_install_mode">
        <td class="text-center text-nowrap">
  
  <code class="language-meson">install_mode</code>
  <br>{meson:since}`1.7.0`
  </td>
        <td class="text-center">
  
  [[@array]][<span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@int]]</span>]
  </td>
        <td>
  
  Specify the file mode in symbolic format
and optionally the owner/uid and group/gid for the installed files.

See the `install_mode` kwarg of [[install_data]] for more information.

  </td>
      </tr>
      <tr id="vcs_tag_install_tag">
        <td class="text-center text-nowrap">
  
  <code class="language-meson">install_tag</code>
  <br>{meson:since}`1.7.0`
  </td>
        <td class="text-center">
  
  <span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span>
  </td>
        <td>
  
  A string used by the `meson install --tags` command
to install only a subset of the files. By default the file has no install
tag which means it is not being installed when `--tags` argument is specified.

  </td>
      </tr>
      <tr id="vcs_tag_output">
        <td class="text-center text-nowrap">
  
  <code class="language-meson">output</code>
  </td>
        <td class="text-center">
  
  <span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span>
  </td>
        <td>
  
  File to write the results to (e.g. `version.c`).
  </td>
      </tr>
      <tr id="vcs_tag_replace_string">
        <td class="text-center text-nowrap">
  
  <code class="language-meson">replace_string</code>
  </td>
        <td class="text-center">
  
  <span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span>
  <br><code class="language-meson text-nowrap">default = '@VCS_TAG@'</code>
  </td>
        <td>
  
  String in the input file to substitute with the commit information.
  </td>
      </tr>
    </tbody>
  </table>

## warning()

This function prints its argument to stdout prefixed with WARNING:.

<p style="padding: 5px; margin: 0px;"></p> <!-- A bit of space because we remove the top margin below -->
<div style="display: flex;">
  <h3 style="margin-top: 0px;">Signature</h3>
  <div style="flex-grow: 1;"></div>
  <div class="d-flex flex-column align-items-center gap-1">

{meson:since}`0.44.0`
</div>
</div>

<pre><code class="language-meson"># This function prints its argument to stdout prefixed with WARNING:
[[#@void]] warning(
  [[#@str]] | [[#@int]] | [[#@bool]] | [[#@array]][[[#@str]] | [[#@int]] | [[#@bool]]] | [[#@dict]][[[#@str]] | [[#@int]] | [[#@bool]]]                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          <a href="#warning_text"><b>text</b></a>,          # The message to print
  [[#@str]] | [[#@int]] | [[#@bool]] | [[#@array]][[[#@str]] | [[#@int]] | [[#@bool]]] | [[#@dict]][[[#@str]] | [[#@int]] | [[#@bool]]]                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          <b>more_text</b>...,  # Additional text that will be printed separated by spaces
)</code></pre>




  <p style="padding: 5px; margin: 0px;"></p> <!-- A bit more space -->

  ### Arguments

  [Argument flattening](Syntax.md#argument-flattening) is **NOT SUPPORTED** by this function.

  The function `warning()` accepts the following positional arguments:

  <table class="table table-bordered table-sm">
    <thead>
      <tr>
        <th class="text-center text-nowrap">Name</th>
        <th class="text-center text-nowrap">Type</th>
        <th>Description</th>
      </tr>
    </thead>
    <tbody>
      <tr id="warning_text">
        <td class="text-center text-nowrap">
  
  <code class="language-meson">text</code>
  </td>
        <td class="text-center">
  
  <span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@int]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@bool]]</span> | [[@array]][<span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@int]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@bool]]</span>] | [[@dict]][<span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@int]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@bool]]</span>]
  </td>
        <td>
  
  The message to print.
  </td>
      </tr>
    </tbody>
  </table>

  <p style="padding: 5px; margin: 0px;"></p> <!-- Extra space -->

  Additionally, the
  function accepts between `0` and `infinity` variadic
  arguments (`more_text...`) of type <code><span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@int]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@bool]]</span> | [[@array]][<span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@int]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@bool]]</span>] | [[@dict]][<span class="text-nowrap">[[@str]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@int]]</span> | <span class="text-nowrap">[[@bool]]</span>]</code>.

  Additional text that will be printed separated by spaces.

  {meson:since}`0.54.0`

  <p style="padding: 5px; margin: 0px;"></p> <!-- Extra space -->


<p style="padding: 7.5px; margin: 0px;"></p>
